Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the cycle object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 860. The moduli space has dimension at most 613 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by cycle generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 860, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 613. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the cycle framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 707. The resulting operator achieves bound O(466/n^5).

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
